([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-classified-the-limitation-of-lia-c7ca29beadd697d8))
- **agent-classified-the-same-bank-fee-thr-9702a4996323240d**: Fixes an agent that classified the same bank fee three different ways across three runs and the variance report failed its check: pin fee classification to a deterministic mapping table, reclassify the inconsistent history, and add a stability test to the agent run. Use for agent categorisation in recs. Not for a fee whose correct classification genuinely changed by policy. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-classified-the-same-bank-fee-thr-9702a4996323240d))
- **agent-classified-tips-as-tax-every-res-d82b6a322443a125**: Fixes an audit agent that misclassifies tip lines as tax by parsing receipt line labels instead of position. Use when an agent classifies tips as tax on restaurant receipts. Not for receipts where tip and tax are genuinely ambiguous. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-classified-tips-as-tax-every-res-d82b6a322443a125))
- **agent-classifier**: Бинарная классификация LLM-системы как АГЕНТ или НЕ-АГЕНТ по исходному коду и трейсам выполнения (логи, OpenTelemetry, LangSmith, JSONL). Используй этот навык ВСЕГДА, когда нужно: (1) определить, является ли система агентом или обычным LLM-приложением/workflow/пайплайном; (2) провести аудит репозитория или трейсов на «агентность»; (3) выдать вердикт AGENT / NOT-AGENT с доказательствами для команды разработки. Триггеры — фразы «это агент или нет», «оцени агентность», «классифицируй по коду/трейсам», «аудит LLM-приложения», упоминание LangGraph, AgentExecutor, CrewAI, AutoGen, tool use в контек… ([romannekrasovaillm/agent-eval-skills](https://github.com/romannekrasovaillm/agent-eval-skills/tree/HEAD/agent-classifier))
- **agent-claude-code-extension-builder**: Construction d'extensions et skills pour Claude Code — slash commands, hooks, intégration MCP et commandes personnalisées. Se déclenche avec "extension Claude Code", "skill Claude", "slash command", "Claude Code plugin", "custom command Claude". Also triggers on "build a Claude Code plugin", "custom slash command", "Claude Code hooks". ([vantaflow/claude-skills-collection](https://github.com/vantaflow/claude-skills-collection/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-claude-code-extension-builder) · ★30 · 2 copies)

- **agent-clean**: Purge tasks from an autonomous coding agent that failed due to infrastructure errors (rate limits, credit exhaustion, auth failures, network) — not code failures. Cleans the queue without losing legitimate failures that need triage. ([Ormus-Solutions/ormus-agent-ops](https://github.com/Ormus-Solutions/ormus-agent-ops/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-clean))
- **agent-clean-code**: Optimizes code structure and documentation for AI agent readability and reasoning. Use when refactoring codebases for agent-assisted development, setting up project rules like CLAUDE.md or AGENTS.md, or reviewing agent-generated code. ([lekodeveloperweb/extrapowers](https://github.com/lekodeveloperweb/extrapowers/tree/HEAD/engineering/agent-clean-code))
